                applicable, include Age Group. If the value is Child, list Youth or Kids. If the value is Adult, it's unnecessary to list, but is ok if desired. 4. If applicable. Max of 2 values in the
                product name. 5. Use Sport OR Activity OR Body Part. Sport can be used in combination with Activity or Body Part. At least one of these attributes need to be used. Ex:
                Strike 6. If applicable. Omit if the value = Single Piece. If the item is part of a set/kit/system, list it here. 7. If applicable. Only include if there is a value present for Items
                Included. If someone is included with the item, list it as "with" Items Included or "and" Items Included. Ex: with Sparring Helmet 8. Only include Count "Pack" if Count is
                greater than 1. Ex: 2 Pack. If the item is part of a kit/set/system and the items aren't identical, use Piece Count "Piece". Ex: 3 Piece

               Per Pack9 + "Count"), + MultiPack Quantity10 + "Pack"

                values 4. if applicable, if value = Round, Square, Octagon, list and Omit all other values 5. Singular 6. Required, Omit if Value = Single Piece. 7. If applicable, maximum
                value of 2, add "w/" in front of Accessories Included and separate with "&". If more than 2 values list as w/ Accessories Included. 8. Required, If more than 1 color, Separate

                conjunction with Brand. Ex: Disney by Sterlite. 2. If applicable, maximum value of 1, otherwise omit 3. If applicable, maximum value of 1 4. Required, if value = Aluminum,
                Steel, Metal, Leather or Faux Leather, List and Omit all other values 5. Required, maximum value of 1 6. Singular or Plural 7. If applicable, maximum value of 1, add "Holds"
                in front of Number of Darts, followed by "Darts". 8. If applicable, maximum value of 1, either Theme or Pattern but not both 9. Required, If more than 1 color, Separate with
                "&", if more than 2 Colors list as Multi-color . If Character Group, Character, Sports Team, or Sports League, Omit Color 10. Only use if Count is greater than 1 11. Multipack
                quantity if > than 1

                conjunction with Brand. Ex: Disney by Sterlite. 2. If applicable, otherwise Omit. 3. If applicable, maximum value of 1. 4. If applicable, if Dart Flight Type = Standard or Slim,
                list and omit all other values 5. Plural or Singular 6. Required, Omit if value = Single Piece. 7. If applicable, maximum value of 1, add "w/" in front of Accessories Included. If
                more than 1 value list as w/ Accessories Included. 8. If applicable, If more than 1 color, Separate with "&", if more than 2 Colors list as Multi-color. 9. Only use if Count is
                greater than 1 10. Multipack quantity if > than 1

EXAMPLE 1. "Dartfellas Santa Claus Season's Greetings Standard 75 Micron Very Strong Dart Flights (3 Count)"
            2. "Winmau Darts Prism Alpha Pro Player Michael Van Gerwen Dart Flights (9 Count)"

FORMULA  Brand + Product Line1 + Sports Team *or* Character *or* Theme2 + Piece Count "-Piece"3 + Features4 + Golf Club Type5 + "Head Cover" + Retail Packaging6, + Color7, +
         Count "Count"8 + (Multipack Quantity "Pack"9)

FOOTNOTES 1. If applicable 2. If applicable; Maximum of 1 Sports Team *or* Character *or* Theme (Ex: Kansas City Chiefs) 3. If applicable; Integer. Add "-Piece" after Value. ONLY list if
                Retail Packaging value is filled out 4. If applicable; Maximum of of 1 Feature 5. Required; Singular; Maximum of 1 Golf Club Type Value. If Feature = Universal, Use "Golf
                Club" 6. If applicable (Ex: Set); Do NOT list if Value = Single Piece 7. Maximum of 1 Color 8. Integer; List only if Value > than 1. Add "Count" after Value. OMIT if Piece Count
                is listed 9. Multipack Quantity if > than 1. Use ( ) around value. Ex: (2 Pack)

EXAMPLE 1. "TaylorMade Dallas Cowboys 12-Piece Universal Golf Club Head Cover Set, Blue"
            2. "Daphne's Tiger Plush Lined Diver Head Cover, Orange, 2 Count (2 Pack)"

ATTRIBUTES Brand  Golf Club Type       Material

FOOTNOTES 1. If applicable. 2. If applicable. If Product Line, Model or Series are the same value, please only use 1 attribute in the name structure. Do not repeat values. 3. Product Type
                = Clubhead

EXAMPLE "Acer SR1 Hybrid Clubhead"

                Required; Add "Flex" after Value 6. Decimal + UOM; Use " for in (Ex: 38.5") 7. Multipack Quantity if > than 1. Use ( ) around value. Ex: (2 Pack)

Golf Complete Sets

               "Pack"8)

                ONLY Values = Women's *or* Men's; If Age Group Value = Child, Change Women's to Girl's and Men's to Boy's 6. Maximum of 2 Values. Add "with" in front. OMIT if needed
                to stay within character count limit. 7. Maximum of 1 Color 8. Multipack Quantity if > than 1. Use ( ) around Value. Ex: (2 Pack)

                Product Width attribute if Golf Hitting & Putting Mat type = Golf Putting Mat or Return Putting Mat. For these items, we just need the Assembled Product Length. 3. Only list
                Power Type if the product is Electronic. Please OMIT from name structure if product is not electronic.

EXAMPLE 1. "SPRI 20 lb Medicine Ball (Black)"
            2. "Go Plus Adjustable 10 - 60 lb Exercise Sandbag (Khaki)"

ATTRIBUTES Brand  Assembled Product Weight      Age Group

Kettlebells

FORMULA  Brand + Product Line (if applicable) + Model (if applicable. If Product Line and Model are the same value, DO NOT list both attributes) + Assembled Product Weight +
         Weight Range (if applicable. If Weight Range is used, DO NOT use Assembled Product Weight Attribute + Finish (if applicable) + Shape (if applicable) + Kettlebell Type + "-" +
         Retail Packaging

FOOTNOTES 1. If Product Line and Model are the same value, DO NOT list both values in naming structure. 2. If Assembled Product Weight is used, DO NOT use Weight Range. If Weight
                Range is used, DO NOT use Assembled Product Weight.

EXAMPLE "GoFit Pro 10 lb Cast Iron Kettlebell"

ATTRIBUTES Brand  Kettlebell Type     Assembled Product Weight

                value. We are looking to list the load capacity value the machine can withhold.
